## Part T — Panorama thumbnails and fold jank (spec §3)

**Goal:** Each row in the View tab's Panoramas list shows a 256×128 JPEG that content-service makes and stores. Rows are no longer given the 4–8K original. A folded list mounts no rows, and folding or unfolding a list does not re-render the three.js scene.

**Architecture:**
- **Catalog.** Catalog owns the DDL, so it adds `panoramas.thumbnail_blob_hash` plus an index. Its `ResolveBlobAccess` treats the new column like `source_blob_hash`.
- **Content-service.**
  - Mounts the shared `blob-data` volume read-write and opens `pkg/blobstore.FS`.
  - A new `internal/thumbnail.Make` decodes the source, scales it to 256×128 with `x/image/draw.ApproxBiLinear`, and `Put`s a q80 JPEG under the sha256 of its bytes.
  - `CreatePanorama` makes the thumbnail before the INSERT. A failure is logged and the row is created without one.
  - A startup goroutine backfills every row whose hash is empty, one at a time, for as long as the process lives.
- **Gateway.** Passes `thumbnailBlobHash` through, and omits it when it is empty.
- **SPA.**
  - The row loads the thumbnail and falls back to the glyph.
  - A folded list keeps its empty `<ul>` and drops its rows.
  - `ViewerCanvas` is wrapped in `memo`. `pageProps` keeps each canvas prop's identity across a fold. It is a pure function, so it memoises the two objects it used to rebuild (`markerLabels`, `move`) with a one-slot `memoLast`.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27.1 (pgx, goose, gRPC, testify/suite + gotest.tools + minimock, testcontainers), `golang.org/x/image` v0.46.0 (new, content-service only), React 19.2 + vitest + Testing Library, Python Playwright + ffmpeg (verification only).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-placement-groups-play-thumbs-design.md` §3. Where this part says "the code wins", implement what it says here.

### Corrections to the spec and the brief (the code wins)

1. **`ResolveBlobAccess` is in catalog-service, not in the gateway.**
   - It lives in `catalog-service/internal/storage/resolve_blob_access.go`.
   - Its integration test is `resolve_blob_access_integration_test.go` beside it.
   - The gateway only calls it through `RequireBlobAccess`, so the gateway needs no change for blob access.
   - The new column joins the existing `panoramas` branch as `(p.source_blob_hash = $1 OR p.thumbnail_blob_hash = $1)`, the same shape `models` already uses. The comment's "six branches" stays true.
2. **Migration 00020 also creates `idx_panoramas_thumbnail_blob`.**
   - `ResolveBlobAccess` runs on every `/api/assets/{hash}` request, and 00014 added an index for exactly this reason.
   - Without the index the new predicate is a sequential scan per asset. With both indexes Postgres answers the OR with a BitmapOr.
3. **The thumbnail is made *before* the INSERT and carried by it. There is no follow-up UPDATE.**
   - This gives one statement and one audit row (the create).
   - It is made once, outside `resolveSlug`, so a slug retry does not decode the source a second time.
   - The spec's "after the insert and stores the hash" would add a second write and a second journal row.
4. **The hash is lowercase sha256 hex of the JPEG bytes.** That is what `upload-service` (`storage/finalize.go` `hashFile`) and `mesh-worker` (`converter/convert.go`) use. `blobstore.Put(ctx, hash, contentType, r)` only checks that the hash is hex. A re-run writes the same key again.
5. **`thumbnail.Make` takes the existing `pkg/blobstore.Store` interface.** No new store interface is added.
6. **The service does not import the thumbnail package.** It takes a `Thumbnailer` func, `func(ctx, srcHash) (string, error)`. Bootstrap closes over `thumbnail.Make` and the FS, so service tests need no filesystem. This makes `service.New(repo, thumb)` take two arguments.
7. **The backfill needs two storage methods, not one:** `ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail` and `SetPanoramaThumbnail`.
   - The UPDATE runs through `audittx.Run`, like every write to `panoramas`.
   - It writes only a row that is still `''`, so it never replaces an existing hash.
   - It does not bump `updated_at`: the SPA's anchor card re-keys on that field, and the change is not an edit anyone made.
8. **Content now needs catalog's migration applied before it boots.** Its SELECT lists name the new column, and the backfill runs only once, at startup. `docker-compose.yml` gives `content` a `depends_on: catalog: service_healthy`. Catalog migrates before it serves, so "healthy" means migrated.
9. **The content Dockerfile pre-creates `/var/blob` as nonroot**, as upload and mesh do. On a fresh volume, a mount point the image lacks comes up `root:root` and the nonroot process cannot write shard directories.
10. **Only two canvas props change identity on a fold: `markerLabels` and `move`.** The hook callbacks are already `useCallback`s; `use-territory-viewer.spec.tsx` pins three of them.
    - `pageProps` is a pure function, not a hook, so `useMemo` is not available there.
    - The two objects, and `groupByModel`'s output, go through `memoLast`. It is a module-level, one-slot, reference-keyed memo.
    - Keeping `groups` stable also stops the Placements panel from getting a new array on every fold.
11. **The folded `<ul>` stays mounted but empty. It is not removed.**
    - The head's `aria-controls` must name an element that exists. That is the reason the doc comment gives for `hidden`, and it still holds.
    - The rows go, which is what the spec is after. The tour's `data-tour="panorama-picker"` anchor is on the `<ul>`, and a running tour forces the fold open anyway.
12. **The spec's "section rows memoised" is skipped.**
    - A row is a few spans, a button and an `<img>` whose `src` does not change, so re-rendering it does not decode anything again.
    - The rows only exist while the list is open.
    - Add it if the "after" trace in TH9 still shows row work on a toggle.
13. **The gateway's `converters.go` is already 207 lines.** `panoramaToAPI` and `documentToAPI` move into a new `content_converter.go`, beside `measurement_converter.go` and `placement_converter.go`, before the field is added.

### Global constraints (every task)

- Branch `feat/placement-groups-play-thumbs`. Other agents share this tree.
  - Never `git stash` / `checkout` / `reset` / `restore`.
  - Never `git add -A` or `git add .`.
  - Never `pkill -f vite`; kill only PIDs you started.
  - To undo an edit, edit the file back.
- To commit: `git add <exact paths>`, then `git commit -m … -- <same paths>`. Check `git diff --cached --name-only` first. New files must be `git add`ed, because `git commit -- <path>` does not pick up untracked files.
- Go gate: `CC=/usr/bin/clang SDKROOT=$(xcrun --show-sdk-path) make -C backend check`. Use the same env on `git commit`, because the pre-commit hook runs it.
  - Integration suites need Docker and are **not** in `make check`. Each task runs its own.
- Frontend gate: `cd frontend && yarn lint && yarn test:coverage && yarn build`.
  - Coverage thresholds are 90/85/90/90.
  - **yarn only.** `yarn lint` is the type check; `tsc --noEmit` checks nothing.
  - Frontend-only commits use `--no-verify` and say so in the message.
  - Every product file has a sibling `*.spec`.
  - Product files ≤ 200 lines, counted by hand without blanks and comments.
- Backend tests use `testify/suite` + `gotest.tools/v3/assert` + minimock, and `s.T().Context()`. Go 1.27 idioms apply: `new(expr)`, `errors.AsType`, `for i := range n`.
- Sentinels live in `domain`. Every write to an audited table goes through `audittx.Run`. A cross-tenant refusal is 404.
- Commit messages end with:
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Ae9nHBFimXZb3sqUigpYwg
  ```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `$SCRATCH/perf/upload-panos.py`.**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Upload each JPEG argument as a panorama of $SLUG through the gateway's chunked
protocol. Bearer auth, so no CSRF token is needed. Prints: id, thumbnail hash (or -), path."""
import json
import os
import sys
import urllib.request

BASE = os.environ.get("BASE", "http://127.0.0.1:8080")
SLUG = os.environ["SLUG"]
CHUNK = 8 << 20  # the SPA's runChunkedUpload size


def call(method, path, token=None, body=None, raw=None, headers=None):
    h = dict(headers or {})
    data = raw
    if body is not None:
        data = json.dumps(body).encode()
        h["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
    if token:
        h["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=data, method=method, headers=h)
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req) as r:
        text = r.read()
        return json.loads(text) if text else None


creds = {"identifier": os.environ.get("LOGIN", "admin"), "password": os.environ.get("PASSWORD", "change-me-now")}
token = call("POST", "/api/auth/login", body=creds)["token"]
for i, path in enumerate(sys.argv[1:], 1):
    blob = open(path, "rb").read()
    up = call("POST", "/api/uploads", token, {"size": len(blob), "contentType": "image/jpeg"})
    for off in range(0, len(blob), CHUNK):
        call("PATCH", f"/api/uploads/{up['id']}", token, raw=blob[off:off + CHUNK],
             headers={"Upload-Offset": str(off), "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream"})
    blob_hash = call("POST", f"/api/uploads/{up['id']}/finalize", token)["hash"]
    pano = call("POST", f"/api/territories/{SLUG}/panoramas", token,
                {"title": f"8K test {i}", "sourceBlobHash": blob_hash,
                 "position": {"x": 0.1 * i, "y": 0, "z": 0}})
    print(pano["id"], pano.get("thumbnailBlobHash", "-"), path)
```

  Run: `cd $SCRATCH/perf && python3 upload-panos.py pano-*.jpg`.
  Expected: eight lines of the form `<id> - pano-N.jpg`. The `-` is correct: no thumbnail exists yet.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `$SCRATCH/perf/fold-trace.py`.**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Record a Chrome trace of $TOGGLES clicks on the viewer's Panoramas fold head.
Report main-thread long tasks (>= 50 ms) and the /api/assets bytes those clicks pulled.
Usage: SLUG=... python3 fold-trace.py <trace.json>"""
import json
import os
import sys

from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

SPA = os.environ.get("SPA", "http://localhost:3000")
SLUG = os.environ["SLUG"]
TOGGLES = int(os.environ.get("TOGGLES", "10"))
out = sys.argv[1]

with sync_playwright() as pw:
    browser = pw.chromium.launch(args=["--ignore-gpu-blocklist"])
    ctx = browser.new_context(viewport={"width": 1440, "height": 900})
    creds = {"identifier": os.environ.get("LOGIN", "admin"), "password": os.environ.get("PASSWORD", "change-me-now")}
    login = ctx.request.post(f"{SPA}/api/auth/login", data=creds)
    assert login.ok, login.text()
    csrf = {"X-CSRF-Token": login.json()["csrfToken"]}
    # A running tour forces the fold open and locks it, and the clicks would do nothing.
    for tour in ("viewer", "panorama"):
        ctx.request.post(f"{SPA}/api/auth/me/onboarding/{tour}", headers=csrf)
    # The route guard reads this marker. The fold starts closed: absence of the key means folded.
    ctx.add_init_script("localStorage.setItem('andrey.authed','1'); localStorage.removeItem('andrey.view.panoramas')")
    page = ctx.new_page()
    assets = []
    page.on("response", lambda r: assets.append(int(r.headers.get("content-length") or 0))
            if "/api/assets/" in r.url else None)
    page.goto(f"{SPA}/territories/{SLUG}", wait_until="load")
    head = page.locator("#view-tab-panoramas button[aria-expanded]")
    head.wait_for(timeout=60_000)
    page.wait_for_timeout(8_000)  # let the scene's LOD download settle before measuring
    assets.clear()
    browser.start_tracing(page=page, path=out,
                          categories=["devtools.timeline", "disabled-by-default-devtools.timeline"])
    for _ in range(TOGGLES):
        head.click()
        page.wait_for_timeout(600)
    browser.stop_tracing()
    browser.close()

trace = json.load(open(out))
events = trace["traceEvents"] if isinstance(trace, dict) else trace
main = {(e["pid"], e["tid"]) for e in events
        if e.get("name") == "thread_name" and e.get("args", {}).get("name") == "CrRendererMain"}
long_ms = [e["dur"] / 1000 for e in events
           if e.get("name") == "RunTask" and (e.get("pid"), e.get("tid")) in main and e.get("dur", 0) >= 50_000]
print(json.dumps({"toggles": TOGGLES, "longTasks": len(long_ms), "maxMs": round(max(long_ms, default=0), 1),
                  "sumMs": round(sum(long_ms), 1), "assetBytes": sum(assets)}, indent=2))
```

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- +goose StatementBegin
-- A 256×128 JPEG that content-service makes from the panorama's equirect, so
-- the View tab's 44×34 row no longer downloads and decodes the whole original.
-- '' means "not made yet": content-service's startup backfill fills it. This is
-- the models.thumbnail_blob_hash convention (00012). The DDL is catalog's
-- although content-service writes the column, like every panoramas change.
--
-- The index serves ResolveBlobAccess, which looks every asset hash up in this
-- column. Without it the new predicate is a sequential scan per request (00014).
ALTER TABLE panoramas
    ADD COLUMN thumbnail_blob_hash TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '';
CREATE INDEX idx_panoramas_thumbnail_blob ON panoramas(thumbnail_blob_hash);
-- +goose StatementEnd

-- +goose Down
-- +goose StatementBegin
DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_panoramas_thumbnail_blob;
ALTER TABLE panoramas
    DROP COLUMN thumbnail_blob_hash;
-- +goose StatementEnd
```

```go
package thumbnail_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"hash/crc32"
	"image"
	"image/color"
	"image/jpeg"
	"image/png"
	"io"
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/suite"
	"gotest.tools/v3/assert"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/pkg/blobstore"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/domain"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/thumbnail"
)

// MakeSuite runs Make against a real FS store in a temp dir: the blob layout
// and the content addressing are part of what is being tested.
type MakeSuite struct {
	suite.Suite
	store *blobstore.FS
}

func TestMakeSuite(t *testing.T) { suite.Run(t, new(MakeSuite)) }

func (s *MakeSuite) SetupTest() {
	store, err := blobstore.NewFS(s.T().TempDir())
	assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
	s.store = store
}

// put stores b under its own sha256, as upload-service does.
func (s *MakeSuite) put(b []byte) string {
	sum := sha256.Sum256(b)
	hash := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
	_, err := s.store.Put(s.T().Context(), hash, "application/octet-stream", bytes.NewReader(b))
	assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
	return hash
}

func gradient(w, h int) image.Image {
	img := image.NewRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, w, h))
	for y := range h {
		for x := range w {
			img.Set(x, y, color.RGBA{R: uint8(x), G: uint8(y), B: 128, A: 255})
		}
	}
	return img
}

// pngHeader is a PNG signature plus a valid IHDR and nothing else. That is all
// DecodeConfig reads, so it can claim any size without the bytes existing.
func pngHeader(w, h uint32) []byte {
	ihdr := binary.BigEndian.AppendUint32(nil, w)
	ihdr = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint32(ihdr, h)
	ihdr = append(ihdr, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0) // 8-bit greyscale, deflate, no filter, no interlace
	chunk := append([]byte("IHDR"), ihdr...)
	out := []byte("\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
	out = binary.BigEndian.AppendUint32(out, uint32(len(ihdr)))
	out = append(out, chunk...)
	return binary.BigEndian.AppendUint32(out, crc32.ChecksumIEEE(chunk))
}

func (s *MakeSuite) TestScalesJPEGAndPNGDownToAStoredJPEG() {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name   string
		encode func(io.Writer, image.Image) error
	}{
		{"jpeg", func(w io.Writer, m image.Image) error { return jpeg.Encode(w, m, nil) }},
		{"png", png.Encode},
	} {
		s.Run(tc.name, func() {
			var src bytes.Buffer
			assert.NilError(s.T(), tc.encode(&src, gradient(512, 256)))

			hash, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, s.put(src.Bytes()))
			assert.NilError(s.T(), err)

			rc, blob, err := s.store.Get(s.T().Context(), hash)
			assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
			defer func() { _ = rc.Close() }()
			body, err := io.ReadAll(rc)
			assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
			sum := sha256.Sum256(body)
			assert.Equal(s.T(), hash, hex.EncodeToString(sum[:]), "content-addressed like every other blob")
			assert.Equal(s.T(), blob.ContentType, "image/jpeg")

			cfg, format, err := image.DecodeConfig(bytes.NewReader(body))
			assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
			assert.Equal(s.T(), format, "jpeg")
			assert.Equal(s.T(), cfg.Width, thumbnail.Width)
			assert.Equal(s.T(), cfg.Height, thumbnail.Height)
		})
	}
}

// The backfill retries a row on every boot, so a rerun must land on the same key.
func (s *MakeSuite) TestARerunWritesTheSameBlob() {
	var src bytes.Buffer
	assert.NilError(s.T(), jpeg.Encode(&src, gradient(512, 256), nil))
	srcHash := s.put(src.Bytes())

	first, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, srcHash)
	assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
	second, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, srcHash)
	assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
	assert.Equal(s.T(), first, second)
}

// The header alone is refused. If the guard slipped, Decode would try to
// allocate the claimed image.
func (s *MakeSuite) TestRefusesASourceWhoseHeaderIsOverTheLimit() {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		name string
		w, h uint32
	}{
		{"too wide", thumbnail.MaxWidth + 1, 1},
		{"too tall", 1, thumbnail.MaxHeight + 1},
		{"a bomb", 100_000, 100_000},
	} {
		s.Run(tc.name, func() {
			_, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, s.put(pngHeader(tc.w, tc.h)))
			assert.ErrorIs(s.T(), err, domain.ErrImageTooLarge)
		})
	}
}

func (s *MakeSuite) TestRefusesWhatIsNotAnImage() {
	_, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, s.put([]byte("%PDF-1.7 not an image")))
	assert.ErrorIs(s.T(), err, image.ErrFormat)
}

func (s *MakeSuite) TestReportsAMissingSource() {
	_, err := thumbnail.Make(s.T().Context(), s.store, "ab"+string(bytes.Repeat([]byte("0"), 62)))
	assert.ErrorIs(s.T(), err, blobstore.ErrNotFound)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement** `internal/thumbnail/make.go`.
```go
// Package thumbnail makes the small JPEG the View tab shows for a panorama,
// so a 44×34 row never downloads and decodes the whole equirect.
package thumbnail

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"image"
	"image/jpeg"
	_ "image/png" // a panorama source is a JPEG or a PNG (the SPA sniffs both)
	"io"
	"sync"

	"golang.org/x/image/draw"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/pkg/blobstore"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/domain"
)

// Width and Height are the thumbnail's size. The source is an equirect, 2:1.
const (
	Width  = 256
	Height = 128
)

// MaxWidth and MaxHeight bound what Make agrees to decode. The header is read
// first, and anything larger is refused before a byte of it is allocated.
const (
	MaxWidth  = 16384
	MaxHeight = 8192
)

const quality = 80

// decoding serialises Make. A source at the limit decodes to ~400 MB, and a
// create racing the backfill, or two creates, would hold two at once.
// ponytail: one decode per process; a semaphore of N if uploads ever queue.
var decoding sync.Mutex

// Make decodes the image blob srcHash and scales it to Width×Height. It stores
// the result as a JPEG and returns the hash, which is the lowercase sha256 hex
// of the JPEG bytes: the addressing upload-service and mesh-worker use. A rerun
// therefore rewrites the same blob instead of adding another.
func Make(ctx context.Context, store blobstore.Store, srcHash string) (string, error) {
	decoding.Lock()
	defer decoding.Unlock()

	src, err := decode(ctx, store, srcHash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	dst := image.NewRGBA(image.Rect(0, 0, Width, Height))
	draw.ApproxBiLinear.Scale(dst, dst.Bounds(), src, src.Bounds(), draw.Src, nil)

	var out bytes.Buffer
	if err := jpeg.Encode(&out, dst, &jpeg.Options{Quality: quality}); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: encode: %w", err)
	}
	sum := sha256.Sum256(out.Bytes())
	hash := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])
	if _, err := store.Put(ctx, hash, "image/jpeg", &out); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: put: %w", err)
	}
	return hash, nil
}

// decode reads the header through a tee and checks the size. It then decodes
// the image from the teed bytes followed by the rest of the stream, so the blob
// is opened once and read once.
func decode(ctx context.Context, store blobstore.Store, hash string) (image.Image, error) {
	rc, _, err := store.Get(ctx, hash)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: get source: %w", err)
	}
	defer func() { _ = rc.Close() }()

	var head bytes.Buffer
	cfg, _, err := image.DecodeConfig(io.TeeReader(rc, &head))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: read header: %w", err)
	}
	if cfg.Width > MaxWidth || cfg.Height > MaxHeight {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: %w: %d×%d", domain.ErrImageTooLarge, cfg.Width, cfg.Height)
	}
	img, _, err := image.Decode(io.MultiReader(&head, rc))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("thumbnail: decode: %w", err)
	}
	return img, nil
}
```

```go
//go:build integration

package storage_test

import (

// thumbnailOf reads the column straight from the table, past every scan helper.
func (s *TerritoryScopeSuite) thumbnailOf(id int64) (hash string, updatedAt time.Time) {

		`SELECT thumbnail_blob_hash, updated_at FROM panoramas WHERE id = $1`, id).Scan(&hash, &updatedAt))

}

func (s *TerritoryScopeSuite) TestCreatePanoramaStoresItsThumbnail() {
	out, err := s.pg.CreatePanorama(s.T().Context(), domain.Panorama{
		TerritorySlug: "a", Slug: "south", Title: "south", SourceBlobHash: "src", ThumbnailBlobHash: "thumb",
	})

// The backfill runs with no caller behind it, so its work list spans every
// territory. It lists only the rows still missing a thumbnail, oldest first.
func (s *TerritoryScopeSuite) TestListPanoramasWithoutThumbnailCrossesTerritories() {
	ctx := s.T().Context()
	insert := func(slug, thumb string) int64 {
		var id int64
		assert.NilError(s.T(), s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO panoramas (territory_id, slug, title, source_blob_hash, thumbnail_blob_hash)
			SELECT id, $1, $1, 'src-' || $1, $2 FROM territories WHERE slug = 'b' RETURNING id`,
			slug, thumb).Scan(&id))
		return id
	}
	insert("done", "thumb")
	pendingB := insert("pending", "")

	got, err := s.pg.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail(ctx)
	assert.NilError(s.T(), err)
	ids := make([]int64, len(got))
	for i, p := range got {
		ids[i] = p.ID
	}
	assert.DeepEqual(s.T(), ids, []int64{s.panoramaA, pendingB})
	assert.Equal(s.T(), got[1].TerritorySlug, "b")
	assert.Equal(s.T(), got[1].SourceBlobHash, "src-pending")
}
```

```go
package storage

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/domain"
)

// ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail returns every panorama on any territory whose
// thumbnail has not been made yet, oldest first. It is the startup backfill's
// work list and crosses territories on purpose: no caller is behind it, and it
// hands nothing back to a reader.
func (r *PG) ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail(ctx context.Context) ([]domain.Panorama, error) {
	const q = `SELECT ` + panoramaSelectCols + `
		FROM ` + panoramaJoin + `
		WHERE pa.thumbnail_blob_hash = ''
		ORDER BY pa.id`

	rows, err := r.pool.Query(ctx, q)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail: query: %w", err)
	}
	out, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(row pgx.CollectableRow) (domain.Panorama, error) {
		return scanPanorama(row)
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("storage.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail: scan: %w", err)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```
  Create `set_panorama_thumbnail.go`:
```go
package storage

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/pkg/audittx"
)

// SetPanoramaThumbnail records the thumbnail made for panorama id.
//
// It writes only a row that still has none, so a backfill racing a create
// never replaces a hash. A deleted row matches nothing, which is not an error.
// updated_at is left alone: the thumbnail is derived from the source, not an
// edit anyone made, and the SPA re-keys the anchor card on it.
//
// It is wrapped in audittx.Run like every write to panoramas. The backfill has
// no actor, so the journal records a system change.
func (r *PG) SetPanoramaThumbnail(ctx context.Context, id int64, hash string) error {
	const q = `UPDATE panoramas SET thumbnail_blob_hash = $2 WHERE id = $1 AND thumbnail_blob_hash = ''`
	err := audittx.Run(ctx, r.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.Exec(ctx, q, id, hash)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("storage.SetPanoramaThumbnail: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
// Thumbnailer turns the image blob srcHash into a thumbnail blob and returns
// its hash. Bootstrap closes over internal/thumbnail and the blob store, which
// keeps the filesystem out of this package and its tests.
type Thumbnailer func(ctx context.Context, srcHash string) (string, error)

// Content is the content service.
type Content struct {
	repo  Repository
	thumb Thumbnailer
}

// New constructs a Content backed by repo, making panorama thumbnails with thumb.
func New(repo Repository, thumb Thumbnailer) *Content { return &Content{repo: repo, thumb: thumb} }
```

```go
package service_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/gojuno/minimock/v3"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/suite"
	"gotest.tools/v3/assert"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/domain"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/service"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/service/mocks"
)

type BackfillSuite struct {
	suite.Suite
	repo *mocks.RepositoryMock
}

func TestBackfillSuite(t *testing.T) { suite.Run(t, new(BackfillSuite)) }

func (s *BackfillSuite) SetupTest() {
	s.repo = mocks.NewRepositoryMock(minimock.NewController(s.T()))
}

var pending = []domain.Panorama{
	{ID: 1, SourceBlobHash: "a"},
	{ID: 2, SourceBlobHash: "undecodable"},
	{ID: 3, SourceBlobHash: "c"},
}

// A source that cannot be decoded is skipped, not fatal: the rest still get
// their thumbnails, and that row is tried again on the next boot.
func (s *BackfillSuite) TestMakesEveryMissingThumbnailAndSkipsAFailure() {
	s.repo.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnailMock.Return(pending, nil)
	var set []string
	s.repo.SetPanoramaThumbnailMock.Set(func(_ context.Context, id int64, hash string) error {
		set = append(set, fmt.Sprintf("%d=%s", id, hash))
		return nil
	})
	thumb := func(_ context.Context, src string) (string, error) {
		if src == "undecodable" {
			return "", errors.New("image: unknown format")
		}
		return "t-" + src, nil
	}

	service.New(s.repo, thumb).BackfillThumbnails(s.T().Context())

	assert.DeepEqual(s.T(), set, []string{"1=t-a", "3=t-c"})
}

// Bounded by the service's lifetime: once the context ends, the next
// panorama is not started.
func (s *BackfillSuite) TestStopsWhenTheContextEnds() {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(s.T().Context())
	defer cancel()
	s.repo.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnailMock.Return(pending, nil)
	s.repo.SetPanoramaThumbnailMock.Set(func(ctx context.Context, _ int64, _ string) error { return ctx.Err() })
	calls := 0
	thumb := func(context.Context, string) (string, error) {
		calls++
		cancel() // shutdown arrives while the first thumbnail is being made
		return "t", nil
	}

	service.New(s.repo, thumb).BackfillThumbnails(ctx)

	assert.Equal(s.T(), calls, 1)
}
```

  add `"log/slog"` to the imports, and append:
```go
// thumbnail makes the row's thumbnail, or answers "". A panorama without one is
// still a panorama: the row shows a glyph, and the startup backfill tries
// again. It is made once, before the slug loop, so a retry does not decode the
// source twice.
func (c *Content) thumbnail(ctx context.Context, srcHash string) string {
	hash, err := c.thumb(ctx, srcHash)
	if err != nil {
		slog.WarnContext(ctx, "content: thumbnail failed", "source", srcHash, "err", err)
		return ""
	}
	return hash
}
```
  Create `backfill_thumbnails.go`:
```go
package service

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/domain"
)

// BackfillThumbnails makes the thumbnail of every panorama that has none. It
// works one panorama at a time, until the list is done or ctx ends. It is also
// the retry for create-time failures, so a source that cannot be decoded is
// tried, and logged once, on every boot. Run it in its own goroutine at startup.
func (c *Content) BackfillThumbnails(ctx context.Context) {
	pending, err := c.repo.ListPanoramasWithoutThumbnail(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		if ctx.Err() == nil {
			slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "content: thumbnail backfill: list", "err", err)
		}
		return
	}
	if len(pending) == 0 {
		return
	}
	slog.InfoContext(ctx, "content: thumbnail backfill started", "pending", len(pending))
	made := 0
	for _, p := range pending {
		if err := c.backfillOne(ctx, p); err != nil {
			if ctx.Err() != nil {
				return // shutting down; the rest waits for the next boot
			}
			slog.WarnContext(ctx, "content: thumbnail backfill failed", "panorama", p.ID, "err", err)
			continue
		}
		made++
	}
	slog.InfoContext(ctx, "content: thumbnail backfill done", "made", made, "failed", len(pending)-made)
}

func (c *Content) backfillOne(ctx context.Context, p domain.Panorama) error {
	hash, err := c.thumb(ctx, p.SourceBlobHash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.repo.SetPanoramaThumbnail(ctx, p.ID, hash)
}
```

```go
package bootstrap

import (
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/pkg/blobstore"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/config"
)

// InitBlobStore opens the shared blob volume. Panorama sources are read from
// it and their thumbnails written back. NewFS creates the root if it is missing.
func InitBlobStore(cfg config.Config) (*blobstore.FS, error) {
	return blobstore.NewFS(cfg.BlobDir)
}
```

```go
package bootstrap

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/pkg/blobstore"
	svc "github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/service"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/storage"
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/content-service/internal/thumbnail"
)

// InitService wires storage and the blob store into the content service. The
// blob store is where panorama thumbnails are made from and written to.
func InitService(pool *pgxpool.Pool, blobs *blobstore.FS) *svc.Content {
	return svc.New(storage.New(pool), func(ctx context.Context, srcHash string) (string, error) {
		return thumbnail.Make(ctx, blobs, srcHash)
	})
}
```

```go
package httpapi

import (
	"github.com/vbncursed/rosneft/backend/services/gateway-service/internal/domain"
)

func panoramaToAPI(p domain.Panorama) Panorama {
	out := Panorama{
		Id:             p.ID,
		TerritorySlug:  p.TerritorySlug,
		Slug:           p.Slug,
		Title:          p.Title,
		SourceBlobHash: p.SourceBlobHash,
		Position:       vec3ToAPI(p.Position),
		YawOffset:      p.YawOffset,
		DefaultYaw:     p.DefaultYaw,
	}
	if !p.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
		out.CreatedAt = &p.CreatedAt
	}
	if !p.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {
		out.UpdatedAt = &p.UpdatedAt
	}
	return out
}

func documentToAPI(d domain.Document) Document {
	out := Document{
		Id:             d.ID,
		TerritorySlug:  d.TerritorySlug,
		Title:          d.Title,
		SourceBlobHash: d.SourceBlobHash,
	}
	if !d.CreatedAt.IsZero() {
		out.CreatedAt = &d.CreatedAt
	}
	return out
}
```
  Check that it still builds: `cd backend/services/gateway-service && go build ./...`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

  `memo-last.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * `fn`, answering its last result again while every argument is the same
 * reference (`Object.is`).
 *
 * `pageProps` is a pure function the viewer calls on every render (a panel
 * fold, a search keystroke), and `ViewerCanvas` is memoised. An object built
 * fresh here would re-render the whole three.js scene for a click that changed
 * nothing in it.
 *
 * ponytail: one slot, module-level. That is right for the one viewer on screen;
 * a second live caller would thrash it (still correct, just uncached).
 */
export function memoLast<A extends unknown[], R>(fn: (...args: A) => R): (...args: A) => R {
  let last: { args: A; result: R } | null = null;
  return (...args: A): R => {
    const prev = last;
    if (prev && prev.args.length === args.length && prev.args.every((a, i) => Object.is(a, args[i]))) {
      return prev.result;
    }
    const result = fn(...args);
    last = { args, result };
    return result;
  };
}
```
  `page-props-b.tsx`:
  - Import `type Vec3` from `@/entities/placement` alongside the existing names, and `import { memoLast } from "./memo-last";`.
  - After `markerLabels` (line 43), add:
```tsx
// Memoised: pageProps runs on every page render, and a fresh object in these two
// canvas props re-rendered the 3D scene on every fold (`memo-last.ts`).
const labelsOf = memoLast(markerLabels);
const moveOf = memoLast((active: boolean, draggingId: number | null, livePos: Vec3 | null) => ({
  active,
  draggingId,
  livePos,
}));
```
  - In `panoramaCanvasProps`, change the two lines to:
```tsx
    markerLabels: labelsOf(groups),
    move: moveOf(p.mode.move, pan.drag.draggingId, pan.drag.livePos),
```
  `page-props.ts`: add `import { memoLast } from "./memo-last";`, add at module level under the imports
```ts
// The same placements and options give the same groups: the canvas markers and
// the Placements panel both take them, and neither should re-render on a fold.
const groupsOf = memoLast(groupByModel);
```
  and change line 52 to `const groups = groupsOf(p.placements, options);`.

  `viewer-canvas.tsx`: import `memo` from `react`, and turn the component into
```tsx
/**
 * The scene, coloured from the tokens and re-read when the theme flips.
 *
 * The Canvas is a context boundary. Nothing inside it can read the theme, the
 * query client or the permission set, so the three colours three.js needs
 * cross it as a plain prop.
 *
 * Memoised: the page re-renders on every panel fold, tab switch and search
 * keystroke, and each re-render used to reconcile the whole three.js scene. The
 * page keeps every prop's identity across those (`use-territory-viewer.spec`
 * "a folding list" pins it), so the shallow compare is enough.
 */
export const ViewerCanvas = memo(function ViewerCanvas(props: ViewerCanvasProps) {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const [colors, setColors] = useState<SceneColors>(() =>
    readSceneColors(document.documentElement),
  );
  // The tokens live on <html>, which useTheme's own effect restyles — there is
  // nothing to derive during render, only a DOM to re-read afterwards.
  useEffect(() => {
    // oxlint-disable-next-line react/set-state-in-effect
    setColors(readSceneColors(document.documentElement));
  }, [theme]);
  return <SceneCanvas {...props} colors={colors} />;
});
```
  `widgets/viewer-canvas/index.ts` stays as it is: `lazy` resolves to the memo component.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them. Expected: PASS.** Same command → all green.

- [ ] **Step 5: Document the rule.** In `frontend/CLAUDE.md` § "The territory viewer", append to the first bullet ("The Canvas is a context boundary"):
```markdown
  `ViewerCanvas` is `memo`: a page re-render (a fold, a keystroke) must not
  reach `SceneCanvas`, so **every canvas prop keeps its identity unless its
  value changed**. Callbacks are `useCallback`s at their hooks. Objects that
  `pageProps` builds go through `memoLast`
  (`pages/territory-viewer/model/memo-last.ts`), because `pageProps` is pure
  and has no hooks. `use-territory-viewer.spec`'s "a folding list" fails, and
  names the prop, when one does not.
```

  ```bash

    frontend/src/pages/territory-viewer/model/page-props-b.tsx frontend/src/pages/territory-viewer/model/page-props.ts \

    frontend/CLAUDE.md
  git diff --cached --name-only
  git commit --no-verify -m "perf(frontend): a View-tab fold no longer re-renders the 3D scene

ViewerCanvas is memo. pageProps now keeps markerLabels, move and the model
groups by reference through a one-slot memoLast, since it is pure and cannot
useMemo. A hook spec asserts every canvas prop keeps its identity across a fold.
  ```

### Task TH9: after-recording, local backfill and tenant check, production post-deploy checks

**Skills to load:** `example-skills:webapp-testing`, `superpowers:verification-before-completion`, `ponytail:ponytail`, `clean-code`.

**Files:** none in the repo. Output goes to `$SCRATCH/perf/after-trace.json` and `$SCRATCH/perf/after.json`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: TH1's `upload-panos.py`, `fold-trace.py` and `baseline.json`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Rebuild and bring up the three services.** Run from the repo root:

  ```bash
  docker image ls --format '{{.Repository}} {{.CreatedSince}}' | grep -E 'catalog|content|gateway'
  docker compose up -d catalog content gateway
  ```
  Expected: all three images created minutes ago, and all three `running`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Backfill.**
  ```bash

  docker exec -i andrey-postgres-1 psql -U andrey -d andrey -tA <<'SQL'
  SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE thumbnail_blob_hash = ''), count(*) FROM panoramas;
  SQL
  ```
  Expected:
  - `thumbnail backfill started pending=N`, then `thumbnail backfill done made=M failed=F`.
  - `F` equals the number of `thumbnail backfill failed` lines. Seeded panoramas with fake or non-image sources are expected to fail; the eight TH1 uploads must not.
  - The SQL answers `F|<total>`.
  - Restart once (`docker compose restart content`). The log shows the same `F` again, and nothing already made is redone.

- [ ] **Step 3: Size, content type and tenant gate of one thumbnail.**
  ```bash
  H=$(docker exec -i andrey-postgres-1 psql -U andrey -d andrey -tA -c "SELECT thumbnail_blob_hash FROM panoramas WHERE title = '8K test 1'")
  VOL=$(docker volume ls --format '{{.Name}}' | grep 'blob-data$')
  docker run --rm -v $VOL:/b:ro alpine cat /b/${H:0:2}/$H.json; echo
  login() { curl -s -X POST 127.0.0.1:8080/api/auth/login -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"identifier\":\"$1\",\"password\":\"$2\"}" | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin)["token"])'; }
  ROOT=$(login admin change-me-now); OTHER=$(login cotest 'Passw0rd!2026')
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w 'root %{http_code} %{content_type} %{size_download}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $ROOT" 127.0.0.1:8080/api/assets/$H
  curl -s -o /dev/null -w 'other tenant %{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $OTHER" 127.0.0.1:8080/api/assets/$H
  ```
  Expected:
  - Metadata `{"hash":"…","content_type":"image/jpeg","size":…}` with a size around 5–30 KB.
  - `root 200 image/jpeg <same size>`.
  - `other tenant 404`. `cotest` is not assigned the territory, and the thumbnail is under the same scope as the source.

  - **After `up -d catalog content gateway`:**

### Open questions and risks (Part T)

1. **Migration order.** TH2 must land after Part G's `00019`. If a database (the local compose one included) is migrated to 00020 while 00019 does not exist yet, goose later refuses the missing earlier version. Do not run the local stack's catalog between TH2 and G's migration task if they land in the other order.
2. **The production override is untracked.** It could redefine `content` (volumes, env, depends_on), and nobody can read it from here. TH9 step 7 checks the merged config before `up`.
3. **Memory.** A source at the spec's 16384×8192 limit decodes to about 400 MB for a baseline JPEG, about 800 MB for a progressive one (the coefficients are buffered), and 512 MB for 8-bit RGBA PNG.
   - The per-process mutex caps this at one decode.
   - No container memory limit or `GOMEMLIMIT` is set.
   - Insta360 Pro sources are 7680×3840. If the host is small, lower `MaxWidth/MaxHeight` to 8192×4096, which quarters the worst case. This is the user's call; the spec value is kept.
4. **Audit noise.** The backfill's UPDATE is audited: one `panorama.update` system entry per existing panorama on the first boot. Adding `thumbnail_blob_hash` to `v_ignore` would silence it. That needs an audit-service `CREATE OR REPLACE audit_capture()` migration, which Part G's audit migration could carry (coordinate the number). Not done here because the spec does not ask for it.
5. **Blast radius.** content-service now mounts the whole blob store read-write and parses untrusted images. Go's decoders are memory-safe and the header guard bounds allocation, but a compromised content process could now overwrite any blob. It only ever `Put`s.
6. **Create latency.** An 8K panorama's POST gains about 0.5–1.5 s of decode, or waits behind a running backfill decode (the mutex). The upload dialog is already on its progress state then.
7. **Conflicts with Part G.**
   - `page-props.ts:52`: this part wraps `groupByModel` in `groupsOf`. G's `groupPlacements` must stay memoised the same way, and G's new canvas props (hidden placements) must keep their identity, or TH8's "a folding list" test goes red. Apply both in whichever order lands second.
   - `gateway converters.go`: TH6 moves the panorama and document converters out. G edits `sceneBundleToAPI` in the same file, which is a different hunk.
   - `dto.ts`, `catalog.pb.go`: regenerate; never hand-merge.
   - G also introduces a `PlacementGroup` domain type, while `entities/placement/model/groups.ts` already exports `PlacementGroup` (the per-model group that `markerLabels` takes). That clash is G's to resolve.
8. **Calibration still re-renders the canvas.** `usePanoramaCalibration`'s `effective` is rebuilt on each render while an alignment is open, so a Documents fold during calibration still re-renders the canvas (Panoramas is forced open then). This was left alone because it is rare, the scene is live anyway, and a fix would need `useMemo` in the feature hook.
9. **Spec deviation:** section rows are not memoised (correction 12). Revisit only if TH9's trace still shows row work on a toggle.
